# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
1NF is achieved. There are no repeating groups, but there are duplicates for shipID and cruiseID, a ship might have multiple scheduled cruises which is a partial dependency. ^MoAv1XGE

Just make ship with capacity its own table. This removed partial and transitive dependencies, as well as all determinants are candidate keys, as shipID and cruiseID are both candidate keys for their respective relations. ^Q9Eg8IAR
